🤖 RAG ANSWER (44.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus+ Internship Mobility program, you must first
   obtain an acceptance letter from a host organization for a minimum
   internship duration of two months. Undergraduate students must have a
   minimum GPA of 2.20, while graduate students must have a minimum GPA of
   2.5. Applications open at the end of October, and you must submit your CV,
   transcript, and acceptance letter through the Online Application System to
   the Internship Office. Your application will be evaluated based on your
   Erasmus+ Score, which considers your GPA and language proficiency, with
   eligible students ranked accordingly. Selected students must complete and
   submit the Erasmus+ Internship Mobility Agreement, Learning Agreement,
   Grant Agreement, and Travel and Insurance documents, and obtain academic
   approval for the internship content via SUFORM before departure. The
   results are announced by the end of April.
------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------
   Erasmus öğrenim hareketliliğinde hibe, katılımcının yurtdışında geçirdiği
   süreyle hesaplanır; bu süre en az 3 ay veya bir akademik dönem olmalıdır.
   Hibenin günlük tutarı, Ulusal Ajans tarafından belirlenen ve her yıl
   duyurulan ülke bazında geçerli oranlarla çarpılır. Seyahat katkı payı da
   bu tutara eklenir. Katılımcı, hareketlilik süresinin tamamı boyunca mali
   destek alıyorsa, hibe gün sayısı hareketlilik süresine eşittir; sıfır hibe
   alacaksa ise hibe tutarı 0’dır. Ödeme, hibe sözleşmesi imzalanması ve
   öğrenim anlaşması tamamlanmasının ardından, yurtdışına çıkmadan önce
   yapılır.

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (40.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Learning Agreement (LA) process begins when the student, in
   consultation with their Exchange Academic Advisor, prepares and signs the
   LA, specifying courses to be taken abroad and their SU equivalents in ECTS
   credits and course types. The student must submit the LA online within the
   timeline announced by the International Relations Office (IRO), ensuring
   it is approved by the advisor and later signed by the partner institution.
   After obtaining all signatures, the student submits the finalized LA to
   the IRO, which forwards it to the Faculty Administrative Officer for
   approval. The student is responsible for aligning the LA with SU
   graduation requirements and must notify the advisor of any schedule
   changes before the term begins, seeking approval under the LA framework.
   This process applies to both undergraduate and graduate students
   participating in exchange programs.
------------------------------------------------------------

   edilmeyenler 1 yıl boyunca ILL hizmetinden yasaklanır. Kayıp veya hasarlı
   kaynaklar için ödünç veren kurumun kuralları uygulanır ve sorun çözülene
   kadar öğrenci belgesi, transcript ve mezuniyet belgeleri alınamaz.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (44.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Students may object to disciplinary sanctions imposed by supervisors or
   boards within fifteen days by submitting their objection to the University
   Administrative Board. The Administrative Board must conclude the objection
   within fifteen days by either accepting or rejecting the sanction; if
   rejected, the original disciplinary body revises the decision based on the
   grounds provided. Objections to removal from dormitories must be filed
   within five working days and are decided by the Disciplinary Board within
   seven working days. It is also possible to appeal to administrative
   jurisdiction without first using the internal objection process. The
   University notifies relevant units of the final decision within three days
   after the objection is resolved, and students under scholarship are
   reported to the Scholarship Committee within the same timeframe.
------------------------------------------------------------

   resource established to provide support to undergraduate and graduate
   students in need. It is funded through donations from SU employees,
   alumni, students, and external individuals or institutions. Employees can
   contribute by authorizing a monthly deduction from their gross salary at a
   rate they determine, with contributions processed through HR Payroll. The
   fund also accepts donations from international sources via the "Friends of
   Sabancı University Fund," administered through the Turkish Philanthropic

🤖 RAG ANSWER (51.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Disiplin cezası alan öğrencinin burs durumu, cezanın türüne göre değişir:
   Kınama veya bir haftadan bir aya kadar uzaklaştırma cezası alan
   öğrencilerde burs kesintisiz devam eder; bir veya iki dönem uzaklaştırma
   cezası alanlarda, ceza süresi boyunca burs kesilir ve sadece öğrenim
   ücretinin %25’i ödenir; üniversiteye çıkarılma cezası alanlarda burs
   tamamen kesilir ve ceza sonrasında yeniden tahsis edilmez. Disiplin cezası
   alan öğrencilerin bursları, Burs ve Mali Destek Yönergesi çerçevesinde
   Enstitü Yönetim Kurulu (lisansüstü) veya Öğrenci İşleri Komitesi (lisans)
   tarafından değerlendirilir. Ceza bitiminde burs yeniden bağlanabilir,
   ancak üniversiteye çıkarılma cezası alanlarda burs tekrar verilmez.

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (43.5s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To open a graduate program, the Graduate School Board, under the
   coordination of the VP-E, submits an application through YOK’s online
   system with the opinion of the UEC, an Academic Council decision, and
   Board of Trustees approval; education begins only after YOK’s approval. To
   close a graduate program, the Graduate School Board proposes closure with
   justifications, the UEC provides an opinion, the Academic Council reviews
   and may approve, request more information, or reject the proposal, and the
   Board of Trustees makes the final decision; once approved, the closure is
   formally notified to YOK. Information about closed programs is retained in
   the system to preserve student records, and updates to YOKSIS must be
   completed within one week of YOK’s approval. Changes to existing graduate
   programs, such as course requirements or credits, follow the Procedure for
   Opening Courses, Closing Courses, Changes in Courses and Effects of
   Courses on Academic Programs.
------------------------------------------------------------

📊 VERIFIED FACTS (From Knowledge Graph):

[Gpa Calculation]
  • Requirement: alınan tüm dersler GNO hesaplarında kullanılır (lisans)
  • Requirement: all digits after comma are taken into consideration
  • Requirement: last grade for a repeated course is taken into account
  • Requirement: tekrar edilen derslerden alınan en son not dikkate alınır (lisans)
  • Credits: total SU credits of the courses taken in that term (lisansustu)

🔗 RELATIONS (From Knowledge Graph):

  • Sabancı University Grad...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (53.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   GPA at Sabancı University is calculated by dividing the total grade
   points, obtained by multiplying each course’s SU credit by its grade
   coefficient, by the total SU credits of all courses taken in the term. For
   undergraduate students, the Cumulative GPA (CGPA) includes all courses
   taken since enrollment, and the last grade for any repeated course is used
   in the calculation. For graduate students, the same method applies, with
   all digits after the decimal point considered in academic ranking, though
   displayed with two decimals. GPA is computed using only SU credits, as
   defined within the National Credit System.
------------------------------------------------------------

  • faculty member → [responsible for] → pr...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (42.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Students may apply for semester or academic year leave due to health,
   financial, family, personal, academic reasons, or unexpected indispensable
   situations, provided they submit supporting documentation. For
   undergraduate students, applications must be submitted by the last
   business day of the 4th week after the first day of classes to pay 25% of
   the semester fee (or 25% of the annual tuition for a full-year leave);
   applications submitted by the 7th week require payment of 50% of the
   semester fee (or 50% of the annual tuition). Graduate students follow
   similar fee structures but must obtain approval from the related Graduate
   School Administrative Board, while conscripted, arrested, or detained
   students may apply within two months of the event occurring to receive
   retroactive leave. Students on leave who wish to return must petition the
   relevant Graduate School Directorate, and any leave fee paid may be
   deducted from the tuition of the returning semester upon approval.
   Instructor leave requests, handled separately, require submission of a
   Sabbatical Leave Request Form and Project Proposal with deadlines in May
   for fall and October for spring semesters.
------------------------------------------------------------

   doğrultusunda, akademik takvimde belirtilen süre içinde ve başvuruların
   başlamasından 2 hafta önce, Lisans Diploma Programı Değişikliği (İç Yatay
   Geçiş) Başvuru Formu ile geçiş yapmak istediği programın bağlı olduğu
   Fakülte Dekanlığına başvurur. Başvuru koşulları arasında en fazla 93 SÜ
   kredisine sahip olmak ve üniversite ile ilişkisinin kesilmemiş olması yer
   alır. Puan türü aynıysa Fakülte Yönetim Kurulu kararıyla işlem yapılır;
   farklıysa Madde 2.2'deki kriterler aranır. Sonuçlar, ilgili dönemin ders
   kayıtları başlamadan önce öğrencilere duyurulur.

   Zorunlu staj başvurusu yapmak için öğrenci, yaz dönemi sonunda mezuniyet

   irtibata geçerek staj yapacağı yer ve projeyi belirler, ardından OMS
   üzerinden Yeni Staj Projesi Formu’nu doldurur. Staj danışmanını Fakülte
   içerisinden kendisi belirler ve projeyi yazılı olarak onaylatır; onay
   aldıktan sonra Staj Ofisi’ne bilgi verir. SSBF programına kayıtlı ve
   GNO’su en az 3.00 olan öğrenciler, Akademik Staj olarak bir araştırma
   projesinde görev alabilir; bu durumda proje, Mayıs’ın ilk haftasına kadar
   SSBF Dekanının onayını almalıdır. Çift anadal öğrencileri, hem anadal hem
   de çift anadal programı için tek bir ortak projeyi kullanabilir, ancak her
   iki danışmanın da OMS üzerinden onayı gerekir. Uluslararası staj için
   öğrenci, Kariyer Geliştirme ve Staj Ofisine özgeçmiş, transkript ve ek
   belgeleri göndererek başvurur; mülakat sonrası başarılı olanlar staja hak

------------------------------------------------------------
   Disciplinary penalties for students include warning, reprimand, suspension
   from one week to one month, suspension for one or two semesters, and
   dismissal from the university. Warning and reprimand penalties are imposed
   by the relevant Faculty Dean or Graduate School Director, while removal
   from dormitories requires a decision by the Disciplinary Board. Suspension
   and dismissal penalties prohibit students from entering the university
   campus and exclude the suspension period from the maximum study period.
   Students under suspension must pay the tuition for the suspended semester.
   Repetition of a disciplinary offense results in a penalty one grade
   heavier, but dismissal cannot be imposed for repeated offenses.

   the program and monitored each semester.  Diplomas are issued only after

   requirements.

   Found items on campus must be reported to the Security Office in Building
   D2 or by calling extension 3565. Security officers prepare a two-copy
   Found Item Return Form, signed by both the finder and the security
   officer, with the original retained by the Security Directorate and a copy
   given to the finder. Items are numbered upon receipt and classified as
   valuable or non-valuable, then stored in labeled bags. Unclaimed items are
   kept for one year before disposal. Lost items reported by students or
   staff must be notified verbally and in writing to the Security Unit (Tel:
   5555) and the IT Unit if the item is university equipment.

   formatında, başvuru tarihinden en geç bir ay önce alınmış olmalıdır.

   tanımlı kriterlere göre belirlenir; 6 ay veya daha fazla hapis cezası
   almış ve kesinleşmiş mahkumiyeti olan öğrenciler kabul edilmez. Güz
   döneminde yurtta kalan öğrenciler bahar döneminde yeniden başvuru yapmaz,
   ancak yaz döneminde kalmak isteyen tüm öğrenciler bahar dönemi sona
   ermeden 3 hafta önce başvuruda bulunmalıdır. Yurt hakkı kazanan

================
[Generation] Stripped document codes from answer.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (33.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Special event and catering services at Sabancı University are arranged by
   the requesting unit, which must submit the Special Event and Catering
   Request Form on SUform at least 30 days before the event. The Food and
   Beverage Services (FBS) unit then prepares a preliminary offer, including
   menu and specifications, and transmits it to the unit for approval. Upon
   approval, the unit initiates a procurement request, after which the
   Procurement and Operations Unit (POP) receives proposals from approved
   vendors and shares the selected company’s details with the unit and FBS.
   FBS coordinates with the selected company to finalize operational details
   and ensures control and follow-up during the event. These services apply
   to special meals, coffee breaks, cocktails, and cocktail prolongé, and are
   exclusively managed through FBS—units cannot make direct arrangements with
   vendors.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (33.9s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Kampüs servis hizmetleri, Ulaşım Hizmetleri Prosedürüne göre dört ana
   hizmet türüyle düzenlenir: 1) Üniversite Shuttle Servisleri, kampüs ile
   şehir arasında belirlenen saat ve güzergâhlarda çalışır; 2) Kampüs İçi
   Ring Servisi, hafta içi ve hafta sonu farklı saatlerde Ana Kapı’dan
   başlayıp Spor Merkezi, Gösteri Merkezi, DO, YBF Önü, Yurtlar, SUNUM ve
   Shuttle Alanı üzerinden döngüsel seferler yapar; 3) Özel Öğrenci
   Servisleri, öğrenci ile firma arasında koordinasyon sağlanarak sunulur; 4)
   Özel Servis Talepleri, akademik, sanatsal, sporif veya sosyal etkinlikler
   için birimler tarafından ücret karşılığında talep edilebilir. Vardiya

   chest X-ray, complete blood count, Hepatitis A, Hepatitis B, and MMR tests
   for students during enrollment. Students and employees can access
   Psychiatry and Psychology examinations by appointment via phone or email.
   The Health Center provides private health services to students and
   employees through contracted institutions, with referrals required for
   outpatient care but not for inpatient treatment. Health insurance is
   automatically activated for all full-time students (excluding Exchange and
   Project Management Undergraduates) upon enrollment and remains valid until
   graduation. Employees and their spouses/children are covered based on
   their employment contract type. Foreign students have a 40% participation
   rate in the insurance plan. Ambulance services, staffed by a doctor,
   anesthesiologist, and driver, are available for emergencies. All services
   are provided through the Health Center, and health information is recorded
   in the Patient Follow-up Program.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (38.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Araştırma Etik Kurulu (AEK) başvurusu, araştırma projesinin başlangıç
   tarihinden önce ve ilgili fakültenin değerlendirme periyoduna göre
   yapılır. Başvuru, Yürütücü tarafından doldurulur ve Yürütücü öğretim üyesi
   veya eşdeğeri, Araştırmacı ise yüksek lisans veya doktora öğrencisi
   olmalıdır. Başvuru formu, Ulusal (Türkçe veya İngilizce) veya Uluslararası
   formu olmak üzere üç farklı türde hazırlanır ve eksiksiz doldurulmalıdır.
   Başvurular, FİT tarafından EBYS sistemi üzerinden yürütülür; destek
   gerektiren durumlarda ARYİT’e e-posta yoluyla yönlendirilir. Eksiksiz ve
   gönüllülük, gizlilik ve onam formu gibi temel kurallara uygun başvurular,
   AEK toplantısına girmeden PGO tarafından onaylanabilir. AEK kararları
   elektronik ortamda proje yürütücüsüne bildirilir ve onay, onay tarihinden
   itibaren belirli bir süre (örneğin birkaç yıl) geçerlidir.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (43.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Faculty members who have been at Sabancı University for at least six
   months and started their position within the last two years are eligible
   to apply, and may do so only once. Applicants must propose projects
   compatible with their existing research activities and ensure that, by the
   project’s end, they have submitted at least two new documented external
   grant applications or secured one guaranteed external funding source. They
   must also have published high-impact scientific articles. The project
   budget is capped at 300,000 TL for theoretical research and 600,000 TL for
   experimental research, with priority given to infrastructure investments
   over personnel salaries. Projects are considered experimental if
   laboratory equipment, consumables, or field studies exceed 30% of the
   total budget.
